# Error Conditions[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#error-conditions-1)
This is a list of error states and conditions that may be returned by Spark SQL.  
| Error State / SQLSTATE  | Error Condition & Sub-Condition  | Message  |  
| --- | --- | --- |  
| 07001  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#all-parameters-must-be-named)             ` ALL_PARAMETERS_MUST_BE_NAMED   | Using name parameterized queries requires all parameters to be named. Parameters missing names: `<exprs>`.  |  
| 07501  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#invalid-statement-for-execute-into)             ` INVALID_STATEMENT_FOR_EXECUTE_INTO   | The INTO clause of EXECUTE IMMEDIATE is only valid for queries but the given statement is not a query: `<sqlString>`.  |  
| 07501  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#nested-execute-immediate)             ` NESTED_EXECUTE_IMMEDIATE   | Nested EXECUTE IMMEDIATE commands are not allowed. Please ensure that the SQL query provided (`<sqlString>`) does not contain another EXECUTE IMMEDIATE command.  |  
| 07501  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#sql-script-in-execute-immediate)             ` SQL_SCRIPT_IN_EXECUTE_IMMEDIATE   | SQL Scripts in EXECUTE IMMEDIATE commands are not allowed. Please ensure that the SQL query provided (`<sqlString>`) is not SQL Script. Make sure the sql_string is a well-formed SQL statement and does not contain BEGIN and END.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-invoke-in-transformations)             ` CANNOT_INVOKE_IN_TRANSFORMATIONS   | Dataset transformations and actions can only be invoked by the driver, not inside of other Dataset transformations; for example, dataset1.map(x => dataset2.values.count() * x) is invalid because the values transformation and count action cannot be performed inside of the dataset1.map transformation. For more information, see SPARK-28702.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-update-field)             ` CANNOT_UPDATE_FIELD   | Cannot update `<table>` field `<fieldName>` type:  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-update-field-array-type)             ` ARRAY_TYPE   | Update the element by updating `<fieldName>`.element.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-update-field-interval-type)             ` INTERVAL_TYPE   | Update an interval by updating its fields.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-update-field-map-type)             ` MAP_TYPE   | Update a map by updating `<fieldName>`.key or `<fieldName>`.value.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-update-field-struct-type)             ` STRUCT_TYPE   | Update a struct by updating its fields.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#cannot-update-field-user-defined-type)             ` USER_DEFINED_TYPE   | Update a UserDefinedType[`<udtSql>`] by updating its fields.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#class-unsupported-by-map-objects)             ` CLASS_UNSUPPORTED_BY_MAP_OBJECTS   | `MapObjects` does not support the class `<cls>` as resulting collection.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#column-array-element-type-mismatch)             ` COLUMN_ARRAY_ELEMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH   | Some values in field `<pos>` are incompatible with the column array type. Expected type `<type>`.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#concurrent-query)             ` CONCURRENT_QUERY   | Another instance of this query was just started by a concurrent session.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#corrupted-catalog-function)             ` CORRUPTED_CATALOG_FUNCTION   | Cannot convert the catalog function '`<identifier>`' into a SQL function due to corrupted function information in catalog. If the function is not a SQL function, please make sure the class name '`<className>`' is loadable.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#create-permanent-view-without-alias)             ` CREATE_PERMANENT_VIEW_WITHOUT_ALIAS   | Not allowed to create the permanent view `<name>` without explicitly assigning an alias for the expression `<attr>`.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#datatype-cannot-order)             ` DATATYPE_CANNOT_ORDER   | Type `<dataType>` does not support ordered operations.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#define-flow-once-option-not-supported)             ` DEFINE_FLOW_ONCE_OPTION_NOT_SUPPORTED   | Defining a one-time flow `<flowName>` with the 'once' option is not supported.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#describe-json-not-extended)             ` DESCRIBE_JSON_NOT_EXTENDED   | DESCRIBE TABLE ... AS JSON only supported when [EXTENDED|FORMATTED] is specified. For example: DESCRIBE EXTENDED `<tableName>` AS JSON is supported but DESCRIBE `<tableName>` AS JSON is not.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#distinct-window-function-unsupported)             ` DISTINCT_WINDOW_FUNCTION_UNSUPPORTED   | Distinct window functions are not supported: `<windowExpr>`.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#empty-schema-not-supported-for-datasource)             ` EMPTY_SCHEMA_NOT_SUPPORTED_FOR_DATASOURCE   | The `<format>` datasource does not support writing empty or nested empty schemas. Please make sure the data schema has at least one or more column(s).  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#expression-translation-to-v2-is-not-supported)             ` EXPRESSION_TRANSLATION_TO_V2_IS_NOT_SUPPORTED   | Expression `<expr>` cannot be translated to v2 expression.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#invalid-pandas-udf-placement)             ` INVALID_PANDAS_UDF_PLACEMENT   | The group aggregate pandas UDF `<functionList>` cannot be invoked together with as other, non-pandas aggregate functions.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#invalid-partition-column-data-type)             ` INVALID_PARTITION_COLUMN_DATA_TYPE   | Cannot use `<type>` for partition column.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#lateral-join-with-arrow-udtf-unsupported)             ` LATERAL_JOIN_WITH_ARROW_UDTF_UNSUPPORTED   | LATERAL JOIN with Arrow-optimized user-defined table functions (UDTFs) is not supported. Arrow UDTFs cannot be used on the right-hand side of a lateral join. Please use a regular UDTF instead, or restructure your query to avoid the lateral join.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability)             ` MISSING_CATALOG_ABILITY   | Catalog `<plugin>` does not support  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-create-function)             ` CREATE_FUNCTION   | CREATE FUNCTION.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-drop-function)             ` DROP_FUNCTION   | DROP FUNCTION.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-functions)             ` FUNCTIONS   | functions.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-namespaces)             ` NAMESPACES   | namespaces.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-procedures)             ` PROCEDURES   | procedures.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-refresh-function)             ` REFRESH_FUNCTION   | REFRESH FUNCTION.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-tables)             ` TABLES   | tables.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-table-valued-functions)             ` TABLE_VALUED_FUNCTIONS   | table-valued functions.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#missing-catalog-ability-views)             ` VIEWS   | views.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#multipart-flow-name-not-supported)             ` MULTIPART_FLOW_NAME_NOT_SUPPORTED   | Flow with multipart name '`<flowName>`' is not supported.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#multi-udf-interface-error)             ` MULTI_UDF_INTERFACE_ERROR   | Not allowed to implement multiple UDF interfaces, UDF class `<className>`.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#named-parameter-support-disabled)             ` NAMED_PARAMETER_SUPPORT_DISABLED   | Cannot call function `<functionName>` because named argument references are not enabled here. In this case, the named argument reference was `<argument>`. Set "spark.sql.allowNamedFunctionArguments" to "true" to turn on feature.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#negative-scale-disallowed)             ` NEGATIVE_SCALE_DISALLOWED   | Negative scale is not allowed: '`<scale>`'. Set the config `<sqlConf>` to "true" to allow it.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-change-column)             ` NOT_SUPPORTED_CHANGE_COLUMN   | ALTER TABLE ALTER/CHANGE COLUMN is not supported for changing `<table>`'s column `<originName>` with type `<originType>` to `<newName>` with type `<newType>`.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-change-same-column)             ` NOT_SUPPORTED_CHANGE_SAME_COLUMN   | ALTER TABLE ALTER/CHANGE COLUMN is not supported for changing `<table>`'s column `<fieldName>` including its nested fields multiple times in the same command.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-command-for-v2-table)             ` NOT_SUPPORTED_COMMAND_FOR_V2_TABLE   | `<cmd>` is not supported for v2 tables.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-command-without-hive-support)             ` NOT_SUPPORTED_COMMAND_WITHOUT_HIVE_SUPPORT   | `<cmd>` is not supported, if you want to enable it, please set "spark.sql.catalogImplementation" to "hive".  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-in-jdbc-catalog)             ` NOT_SUPPORTED_IN_JDBC_CATALOG   | Not supported command in JDBC catalog:  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-in-jdbc-catalog-command)             ` COMMAND   | `<cmd>`  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#not-supported-in-jdbc-catalog-command-with-property)             ` COMMAND_WITH_PROPERTY   | `<cmd>` with property `<property>`.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#pipeline-dataset-without-flow)             ` PIPELINE_DATASET_WITHOUT_FLOW   | Pipeline dataset `<identifier>` does not have any defined flows. Please attach a query with the dataset's definition, or explicitly define at least one flow that writes to the dataset.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#pipe-operator-aggregate-expression-contains-no-aggregate-function)             ` PIPE_OPERATOR_AGGREGATE_EXPRESSION_CONTAINS_NO_AGGREGATE_FUNCTION   | Non-grouping expression `<expr>` is provided as an argument to the |> AGGREGATE pipe operator but does not contain any aggregate function; please update it to include an aggregate function and then retry the query again.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#pipe-operator-contains-aggregate-function)             ` PIPE_OPERATOR_CONTAINS_AGGREGATE_FUNCTION   | Aggregate function `<expr>` is not allowed when using the pipe operator |> `<clause>` clause; please use the pipe operator |> AGGREGATE clause instead.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#scalar-subquery-is-in-group-by-or-aggregate-function)             ` SCALAR_SUBQUERY_IS_IN_GROUP_BY_OR_AGGREGATE_FUNCTION   | The correlated scalar subquery '`<sqlExpr>`' is neither present in GROUP BY, nor in an aggregate function. Add it to GROUP BY using ordinal position or wrap it in `first()` (or `first_value`) if you don't care which value you get.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#star-group-by-pos)             ` STAR_GROUP_BY_POS   | Star (*) is not allowed in a select list when GROUP BY an ordinal position is used.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode)             ` STREAMING_REAL_TIME_MODE   | Streaming real-time mode has the following limitation:  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode-async-progress-tracking-not-supported)             ` ASYNC_PROGRESS_TRACKING_NOT_SUPPORTED   | Async progress tracking is not supported in real-time mode. Set option asyncProgressTrackingEnabled to false and retry your query.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode-identical-sources-in-union-not-supported)             ` IDENTICAL_SOURCES_IN_UNION_NOT_SUPPORTED   | Real-time mode does not support union on two or more identical streaming data sources in a single query. This includes scenarios such as referencing the same source DataFrame more than once, or using two data sources with identical configurations for some sources. For Kafka, avoid reusing the same DataFrame and create different ones. Sources provided in the query: `<sources>`  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode-input-stream-not-supported)             ` INPUT_STREAM_NOT_SUPPORTED   | The input stream `<className>` is not supported in Real-time Mode.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode-operator-or-sink-not-in-allowlist)             ` OPERATOR_OR_SINK_NOT_IN_ALLOWLIST   | The `<errorType>`(s): `<message>` not in the `<errorType>` allowlist for Real-Time Mode. To bypass this check, set spark.sql.streaming.realTimeMode.allowlistCheck to false. By changing this, you agree to run the query at your own risk.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode-output-mode-not-supported)             ` OUTPUT_MODE_NOT_SUPPORTED   | The output mode `<outputMode>` is not supported. To work around this limitation, set the output mode to Update. In the future, `<outputMode>` may be supported.  |  
|   |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#streaming-real-time-mode-sink-not-supported)             ` SINK_NOT_SUPPORTED   | The `<className>` sink is currently not supported. See the Real-Time Mode User Guide for a list of supported sinks.  |  
| 0A000  |  `             #[](https://spark.apache.org/docs/latest/sql-error-conditions.html#table-valued-arguments-not-yet-implemented-for-sql-functions)             ` TABLE_VALUED_ARGUMENTS_NOT_YET_IMPLEMENTED_FOR_SQL_FUNCTIONS   | Cannot `<action>` SQL user-defined function `<functionName>` with TABLE arguments because this functionality is not yet implemented.  |
